### Alga Sušak, a pharmaceutical and cosmetics company / manufacturer in Sušak, Croatia; Economic history of Sušak, Croatia.

- verdict: **invalid** (confidence 0.95) · Malformed string: descriptive note text (not a heading) combining a corporate name with a descriptive phrase and a second, separate heading joined by a semicolon
- The string is not a single valid LCSH heading: it mixes a descriptive note, a slash, and a semicolon-joined second statement about economic history of a place. The corporate name 'Alga Sušak' was not found in the authority file, and the place 'Sušak' is not an established geographic heading (the search returned only Susak Island (Croatia)). Under H 1105 and H 1075, a corporate name is given without descriptive phrases, and each subject heading is a separate field with its own subdivisions. The string must be split into separate headings and corrected against authority records before it can be used.
- [error] whole string: The supplied string is a descriptive note (a corporate description with a slash and an appended statement about economic history) combining two distinct headings with a semicolon; a subject heading string must be a single heading with subdivisions, and the semicolon/second heading cannot be expressed in one 650/610 field. (H 1075 sec. E; H 1095 sec. A; Appendix D sec. 1). Fix: Split into two separate headings, each validated on its own, and remove descriptive wording from the corporate name.
- [error] Alga Sušak, a pharmaceutical and cosmetics company / manufacturer in Sušak, Croatia: Descriptive phrase and slash are not permitted in a name heading; no LCNAF or LCSH record exists for 'Alga Sušak'. Descriptive information belongs in a 678 or 520 note, not in the heading. (H 1105; H 430; Appendix D sec. 4). Fix: Use the authorized corporate name (if established in LCNAF) without description, or treat as uncertain pending verification of the name record.
- [error] Economic history of Sušak, Croatia: Inverted/phrase form with an unestablished place name; the place 'Sušak' is not an authorized geographic heading, and 'Economic history' is written as a phrase rather than as a subdivision. (H 830 sec. C.1, C.5; H 1140; H 1095 sec. E.2). Fix: Use Alga Sušak (corporate name, if established) followed by the free-floating topical subdivision --Economic conditions or --History as appropriate, and for the place use the established form Susak Island (Croatia) only if the place is really meant, or propose the city heading.
- alternatives: Alga Sušak (Firm)--Economic conditions (if the corporate name is established and the subdivision is valid under H 1105); Economic history--Croatia--Sušak (only if a city heading Sušak (Croatia) is established; currently not found)

### United States--Public opinion

- verdict: **invalid** (confidence 0.93) · Geographic (jurisdiction) main heading with topical subdivision; subdivision Public opinion is not authorized directly under place names
- The main heading United States is an authorized jurisdiction heading (n78095330) and may stand as a main heading. The subdivision Public opinion is established in the authority file as a topical subdivision, but its usage statement (H 1095, H 1100, H 1103, H 1105, H 1110, H 1200) covers persons, corporate bodies, classes of persons, ethnic groups, wars and topics, not place names. H 1955 sec. 3.a explicitly says --Public opinion is not to be used directly under names of places; an intervening topical subdivision is required (for example, United States--Politics and government--Public opinion). The string as written therefore breaks that prohibition. The reversed form Public opinion--United States, as described in H 1955 sec. 1.b, is the authorized way to express public opinion within the United States.
- [error] Public opinion: The subdivision --Public opinion is used directly under the name of a country (United States). H 1955 sec. 3.a says not to use --Public opinion directly under names of places; a topical subdivision must be interposed. Public opinion about a place is also not expressed by [place]--Public opinion; the place form is Public opinion--[place] (H 1955 sec. 1.b). (H 1955 sec. 3.a; H 1955 sec. 1.b; H 1955 sec. 3.a note on --Public opinion not qualified by place names; H 1095 sec. F usage statement (no place names)). Fix: Use Public opinion--United States, where the place identifies the location of the people holding the opinion (H 1955 sec. 1.b), or interpose a topic, e.g. United States--[topic]--Public opinion (H 1955 sec. 3.a).
- alternatives: Public opinion--United States; United States--Politics and government--Public opinion; United States--Social conditions--Public opinion

### 1400-1799

- verdict: **invalid** (confidence 0.92) · Bare date span (chronological expression) offered as a main heading
- The supplied string consists only of a numeric date span with no heading to which it is attached. Under H 620 sec. 1.e.(1) a date span without words is an accepted form of a chronological subdivision, which follows a main heading; it does not stand alone as a heading. The authority-file search found no established heading or whole string '1400-1799'. Because this is a structural defect established by the rules and the absence of any authorized heading, the verdict is invalid. A cataloger needs to supply a topical, geographic, or name heading to which the span is subdivided, or substitute an established period heading in words.
- [error] 1400-1799: A bare date span is being used as a main heading. Chronological expressions are authorized only as subdivisions following a topical, geographic, or name heading, or as general period headings written in words (e.g., 'Renaissance', 'Eighteenth century'). Nothing in LCSH authorizes a numeric date span standing alone. (H 620 sec. 1.e.(1) and sec. 2.c; H 620 sec. 3.b (numerals in chronological subdivisions only after a heading)). Fix: Attach the span as a chronological subdivision after an established heading that takes $y, e.g. 'Music--Italy--1400-1799' only if the topic is valid under that heading, or use a general period heading in words such as 'Renaissance' or 'Early modern period' as appropriate to the work's content.
- alternatives: Renaissance; Renaissance--Europe; Europe--History--1400-1799

- The main heading Moldova is established through the LCNAF record n90720192, which replaces the deprecated LCSH heading sh85086557 and uses the same label. C.1). The string is therefore not invalid under the elements or order. If they are, use Moldova--Foreign relations--Romania. If they are general relations, keep Moldova--Relations--Romania.
- alternatives: Moldova--Foreign relations--Romania (for bilateral diplomatic relations; add reciprocal Romania--Foreign relations--Moldova); Romania--Relations--Moldova (reciprocal heading to accompany Moldova--Relations--Romania)
